{"parentUuid":"9e5b32f5-0256-437f-9a72-487059765f3d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"a18f2b8332658cbb6","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015LSfWpxWD8EhAywFBCWZ9g","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t// SERVER-SIDE STT MODEL REGISTRY\n2\t// Single source of truth for which (provider, model) pairs HyperWhisper Cloud\n3\t// will route to, their preflight credit rate, preview status, and vocabulary\n4\t// support. The transcribe route validates every request against this registry\n5\t// BEFORE spending any upstream money, so an unknown or spoofed provider/model\n6\t// is rejected with a 400 rather than silently falling back to a paid default.\n7\t//\n8\t// The client-facing catalog (credits/min captions, language lists, accuracy\n9\t// tiers) lives in `shared-app-classification/cloud-stt-catalog.json`. This file\n10\t// is the backend's narrower, security-critical view: what is actually routable\n11\t// and how to meter it.\n12\t\n13\timport { ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE } from './cost-calculator';\n14\t\n15\texport type SttProviderId =\n16\t  | 'deepgram'\n17\t  | 'groq'\n18\t  | 'elevenlabs'\n19\t  | 'grok'\n20\t  | 'azure-mai'\n21\t  | 'google-chirp'\n22\t  | 'openai'\n23\t  | 'gemini'\n24\t  | 'assemblyai'\n25\t  | 'mistral'\n26\t  | 'soniox';\n27\t\n28\texport interface SttModelDef {\n29\t  /** Upstream model id. Empty string for single-model providers (grok). */\n30\t  id: string;\n31\t  /** Marks a preview/experimental model so clients can badge it. */\n32\t  isPreview?: boolean;\n33\t  /** Whether the upstream honours custom-vocabulary / keyterm biasing. */\n34\t  supportsVocabulary: boolean;\n35\t  /**\n36\t   * Conservative USD-per-audio-minute figure used ONLY for the preflight\n37\t   * credit reservation. Actual billing is computed from the upstream response\n38\t   * (token usage or returned audio seconds) in the provider adapter, so this\n39\t   * just has to be a safe upper-ish bound to gate low-balance abuse.\n40\t   */\n41\t  estimatedUsdPerMinute: number;\n42\t}\n43\t\n44\texport interface SttProviderDef {\n45\t  id: SttProviderId;\n46\t  /** Model used when the caller omits an explicit model. */\n47\t  defaultModel: string;\n48\t  /**\n49\t   * Self-only providers never fall back to a sibling: the caller picked this\n50\t   * model for a reason, so on failure we surface an error rather than silently\n51\t   * substituting a different model/price. All new proxy providers are self-only;\n52\t   * the original cheap trio (deepgram/groq/elevenlabs) keep cross-fallback.\n53\t   */\n54\t  selfOnly: boolean;\n55\t  /** Whether this provider's transcription flow is async (upload + poll). */\n56\t  async: boolean;\n57\t  models: SttModelDef[];\n58\t}\n59\t\n60\t// Medical add-on multiplier surface — only AssemblyAI meters it today.\n61\texport const MEDICAL_DOMAIN = 'medical';\n62\tconst ASSEMBLYAI_MEDICAL_ADDON_USD_PER_MINUTE = 0.15 / 60;\n63\t// Keyterms add-on ($0.05/hr) applies only to universal-3-pro; universal-2 is free/beta.\n64\tconst ASSEMBLYAI_KEYTERMS_ADDON_USD_PER_MINUTE = 0.05 / 60;\n65\t// AssemblyAI's separate sync product (<=120s clips, single blocking request)\n66\t// always runs universal-3-5-pro at its own published rate (\"the same rate as\n67\t// Universal-3.5 Pro Realtime\") — HIGHER than either async tier below and not\n68\t// modeled by a `models[]` entry (sync isn't a selectable model; it's a\n69\t// routing decision the sync-eligibility gate makes for a request that could\n70\t// have asked for either async model). Exported so the preflight reservation\n71\t// (estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks in routes/transcribe.ts) can reserve\n72\t// against it for a request that could route through sync, instead of only\n73\t// ever reserving the lower async catalog rate for the requested model — a\n74\t// short clip (sync's target case) must not be able to deduct more than was\n75\t// reserved. Re-exports cost-calculator.ts's `ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE`\n76\t// (the single source of truth for this rate, used for ACTUAL billing) instead\n77\t// of a second hardcoded copy of the same literal, so the reserved amount can\n78\t// never silently drift from what's actually billed.\n79\texport const ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE = ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n80\t// ElevenLabs keyterm prompting carries a +20% surcharge on base (scribe_v2 only).\n81\tconst ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE = 0.20;\n82\t\n83\tconst PROVIDERS: Record<SttProviderId, SttProviderDef> = {\n84\t  // ── Original cheap trio: cross-provider fallback retained ──\n85\t  deepgram: {\n86\t    id: 'deepgram',\n87\t    defaultModel: 'nova-3-general',\n88\t    selfOnly: false,\n89\t    async: false,\n90\t    models: [\n91\t      { id: 'nova-3-general', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0055 },\n92\t      { id: 'nova-3-medical', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0055 },\n93\t      { id: 'nova-2-general', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0055 },\n94\t      { id: 'nova-2-medical', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0055 },\n95\t    ],\n96\t  },\n97\t  groq: {\n98\t    id: 'groq',\n99\t    defaultModel: 'whisper-large-v3-turbo',\n100\t    selfOnly: false,\n101\t    async: false,\n102\t    models: [\n103\t      { id: 'whisper-large-v3-turbo', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.000667 }, // $0.04/hr ÷ 60\n104\t      { id: 'whisper-large-v3', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.00185 },      // $0.111/hr ÷ 60\n105\t    ],\n106\t  },\n107\t  elevenlabs: {\n108\t    id: 'elevenlabs',\n109\t    defaultModel: 'scribe_v2',\n110\t    selfOnly: false,\n111\t    async: false,\n112\t    models: [\n113\t      { id: 'scribe_v2', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.00983 },\n114\t      // scribe_v1 has no vocabulary biasing — surfaced so clients can badge it.\n115\t      { id: 'scribe_v1', supportsVocabulary: false, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.00983 },\n116\t    ],\n117\t  },\n118\t\n119\t  // ── Single-model self-only providers ──\n120\t  grok: {\n121\t    id: 'grok',\n122\t    defaultModel: '',\n123\t    // grok keeps its historical cross-provider fallback chain\n124\t    // (grok → deepgram → groq → elevenlabs) defined in transcribe.ts.\n125\t    selfOnly: false,\n126\t    async: false,\n127\t    models: [{ id: '', supportsVocabulary: false, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.00167 }],\n128\t Used only for the credit reservation; actual cost is\n261\t * computed from the upstream response in the adapter.\n262\t */\n263\texport function estimatedUsdPerMinute(\n264\t  provider: SttProviderId,\n265\t  model?: string,\n266\t  medical: boolean = false,\n267\t  keyterms: boolean = false,\n268\t): number {\n269\t  const resolution = resolveModel(provider, model);\n270\t  const base = resolution.ok\n271\t    ? resolution.model.estimatedUsdPerMinute\n272\t    : PROVIDERS[provider].models[0].estimatedUsdPerMinute;\n273\t\n274\t  if (provider === 'elevenlabs') {\n275\t    // Keyterm prompting adds +20% on base, scribe_v2 only (scribe_v1 has no biasing).\n276\t    const resolvedModel = resolution.ok ? resolution.model.id : PROVIDERS.elevenlabs.models[0].id;\n277\t    return (keyterms && resolvedModel === 'scribe_v2') ? base * (1 + ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE) : base;\n278\t  }\n279\t\n280\t  if (provider !== 'assemblyai') {\n281\t    return base;\n282\t  }\n283\t\n284\t  const resolvedModel = resolution.ok ? resolution.model.id : PROVIDERS.assemblyai.models[0].id;\n285\t  const medicalAddon = medical ? The transcribe route validates every request against this registry\n// BEFORE spending any upstream money, so an unknown or spoofed provider/model\n// is rejected with a 400 rather than silently falling back to a paid default.\n//\n// The client-facing catalog (credits/min captions, language lists, accuracy\n// tiers) lives in `shared-app-classification/cloud-stt-catalog.json`. This file\n// is the backend's narrower, security-critical view: what is actually routable\n// and how to meter it.\n\nimport { ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE } from './cost-calculator';\n\nexport type SttProviderId =\n  | 'deepgram'\n  | 'groq'\n  | 'elevenlabs'\n  | 'grok'\n  | 'azure-mai'\n  | 'google-chirp'\n  | 'openai'\n  | 'gemini'\n  | 'assemblyai'\n  | 'mistral'\n  | 'soniox';\n\nexport interface SttModelDef {\n  /** Upstream model id. Empty string for single-model providers (grok). */\n  id: string;\n  /** Marks a preview/experimental model so clients can badge it. */\n  isPreview?: boolean;\n  /** Whether the upstream honours custom-vocabulary / keyterm biasing. */\n  supportsVocabulary: boolean;\n  /**\n   * Conservative USD-per-audio-minute figure used ONLY for the preflight\n   * credit reservation. Actual billing is computed from the upstream response\n   * (token usage or returned audio seconds) in the provider adapter, so this\n   * just has to be a safe upper-ish bound to gate low-balance abuse.\n   */\n  estimatedUsdPerMinute: number;\n}\n\nexport interface SttProviderDef {\n  id: SttProviderId;\n  /** Model used when the caller omits an explicit model. */\n  defaultModel: string;\n  /**\n   * Self-only providers never fall back to a sibling: the caller picked this\n   * model for a reason, so on failure we surface an error rather than silently\n   * substituting a different model/price. All new proxy providers are self-only;\n   * the original cheap trio (deepgram/groq/elevenlabs) keep cross-fallback.\n   */\n  selfOnly: boolean;\n  /** Whether this provider's transcription flow is async (upload + poll). */\n  async: boolean;\n  models: SttModelDef[];\n}\n\n// Medical add-on multiplier surface — only AssemblyAI meters it today.\nexport const MEDICAL_DOMAIN = 'medical';\nconst ASSEMBLYAI_MEDICAL_ADDON_USD_PER_MINUTE = 0.15 / 60;\n// Keyterms add-on ($0.05/hr) applies only to universal-3-pro; universal-2 is free/beta.\nconst ASSEMBLYAI_KEYTERMS_ADDON_USD_PER_MINUTE = 0.05 / 60;\n// AssemblyAI's separate sync product (<=120s clips, single blocking request)\n// always runs universal-3-5-pro at its own published rate (\"the same rate as\n// Universal-3.5 Pro Realtime\") — HIGHER than either async tier below and not\n// modeled by a `models[]` entry (sync isn't a selectable model; it's a\n// routing decision the sync-eligibility gate makes for a request that could\n// have asked for either async model). Exported so the preflight reservation\n// (estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks in routes/transcribe.ts) can reserve\n// against it for a request that could route through sync, instead of only\n// ever reserving the lower async catalog rate for the requested model — a\n// short clip (sync's target case) must not be able to deduct more than was\n// reserved. Re-exports cost-calculator.ts's `ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE`\n// (the single source of truth for this rate, used for ACTUAL billing) instead\n// of a second hardcoded copy of the same literal, so the reserved amount can\n// never silently drift from what's actually billed.\nexport const ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE = ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_COST_PER_AUDIO_MINUTE;\n// ElevenLabs keyterm prompting carries a +20% surcharge on base (scribe_v2 only).\nconst ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE = 0.20;\n\nconst PROVIDERS: Record<SttProviderId, SttProviderDef> = {\n  // ── Original cheap trio: cross-provider fallback retained ──\n  deepgram: {\n    id: 'deepgram',\n    defaultModel: 'nova-3-general',\n    selfOnly: false,\n    async: false,\n    models: [\n      { id: 'nova-3-general', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0055 },\n      { id: 'nova-3-medical', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0055 },\n      { id: 'nova-2-general', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0055 },\n      { id: 'nova-2-medical', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0055 },\n    ],\n  },\n  groq: {\n    id: 'groq',\n    defaultModel: 'whisper-large-v3-turbo',\n    selfOnly: false,\n    async: false,\n    models: [\n      { id: 'whisper-large-v3-turbo', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.000667 }, // $0.04/hr ÷ 60\n      { id: 'whisper-large-v3', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.00185 },      // $0.111/hr ÷ 60\n    ],\n  },\n  elevenlabs: {\n    id: 'elevenlabs',\n    defaultModel: 'scribe_v2',\n    selfOnly: false,\n    async: false,\n    models: [\n      { id: 'scribe_v2', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.00983 },\n      // scribe_v1 has no vocabulary biasing — surfaced so clients can badge it.\n      { id: 'scribe_v1', supportsVocabulary: false, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.00983 },\n    ],\n  },\n\n  // ── Single-model self-only providers ──\n  grok: {\n    id: 'grok',\n    defaultModel: '',\n    // grok keeps its historical cross-provider fallback chain\n    // (grok → deepgram → groq → elevenlabs) defined in transcribe.ts.\n    selfOnly: false,\n    async: false,\n    models: [{ id: '', supportsVocabulary: false, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.00167 }],\n  },\n  'azure-mai': {\n    id: 'azure-mai',\n    defaultModel: 'mai-transcribe-1.5',\n    selfOnly: true,\n    async: false,\n    models: [{ id: 'mai-transcribe-1.5', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.006 }],\n  },\n  'google-chirp': {\n    id: 'google-chirp',\n    defaultModel: 'chirp_3',\n    selfOnly: true,\n    async: true,\n    models: [{ id: 'chirp_3', supportsVocabulary: false, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.016 }],\n  },\n\n  // ── New synchronous proxy providers ──\n  openai: {\n    id: 'openai',\n    defaultModel: 'gpt-4o-transcribe',\n    selfOnly: true,\n    async: false,\n    // gpt-4o-* are token-billed (input audio + OUTPUT transcript tokens), so the\n    // preflight rate adds a conservative output allowance on top of the input\n    // floor: ~300 output tokens/min (fast speech ≈ 200 wpm × ~1.3 tok/word, plus\n    // headroom) at the model output rate — $10/1M for transcribe (+$0.003/min),\n    // $5/1M for mini (+$0.0015/min) — so a verbose transcript can't out-bill the\n    // reservation. whisper-1 is duration-billed (no output-token charge).\n    models: [\n      { id: 'gpt-4o-transcribe', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.009 },\n      { id: 'gpt-4o-mini-transcribe', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0045 },\n      { id: 'whisper-1', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.006 },\n    ],\n  },\n  gemini: {\n    id: 'gemini',\n    defaultModel: 'gemini-2.5-flash',\n    selfOnly: true,\n    async: false,\n    // No dedicated vocabulary API — prompt-only biasing, so supportsVocabulary\n    // is false (clients shouldn't promise keyterm accuracy).\n    models: [\n      { id: 'gemini-2.5-flash', supportsVocabulary: false, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0024 },\n      { id: 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite', supportsVocabulary: false, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0008 },\n      { id: 'gemini-2.5-pro', supportsVocabulary: false, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0075 },\n      { id: 'gemini-3-flash-preview', isPreview: true, supportsVocabulary: false, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0030 },\n      { id: 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview', isPreview: true, supportsVocabulary: false, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0100 },\n    ],\n  },\n  mistral: {\n    id: 'mistral',\n    defaultModel: 'voxtral-mini-latest',\n    selfOnly: true,\n    async: false,\n    models: [\n      { id: 'voxtral-mini-latest', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.003 },\n    ],\n  },\n\n  // ── New asynchronous (upload + poll) proxy providers ──\n  assemblyai: {\n    id: 'assemblyai',\n    defaultModel: 'universal-3-pro',\n    selfOnly: true,\n    async: true,\n    models: [\n      { id: 'universal-3-pro', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0035 },\n      { id: 'universal-2', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.0025 },\n    ],\n  },\n  soniox: {\n    id: 'soniox',\n    defaultModel: 'stt-async-v4',\n    selfOnly: true,\n    async: true,\n    models: [\n      { id: 'stt-async-v4', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.00167 },\n      // v5 is API-compatible with v4; v4 auto-routes to it after 2026-06-30.\n      { id: 'stt-async-v5', supportsVocabulary: true, estimatedUsdPerMinute: 0.00167 },\n    ],\n  },\n};\n\nconst PROVIDER_IDS = new Set<string>(Object.keys(PROVIDERS));\n\n// Canonical runtime list of every routable provider. Derived from the registry\n// so it can never drift from it. The deploy smoke test imports this to assert it\n// exercises every provider — a new provider added above can't ship untested.\nexport const ALL_STT_PROVIDER_IDS = Object.keys(PROVIDERS) as SttProviderId[];\n\nexport function isValidProviderId(value: string): value is SttProviderId {\n  return PROVIDER_IDS.has(value);\n}\n\nexport function getProviderDef(provider: SttProviderId): SttProviderDef {\n  return PROVIDERS[provider];\n}\n\nexport type ModelResolution =\n  | { ok: true; model: SttModelDef }\n  | { ok: false; reason: string; validModels: string[] };\n\n/**\n * Resolve and validate a caller-supplied model against a provider. An empty /\n * undefined request resolves to the provider default. An unrecognised model is\n * rejected (fail-closed) — the route turns this into a 400.\n */\nexport function resolveModel(provider: SttProviderId, requested?: string): ModelResolution {\n  const def = PROVIDERS[provider];\n  const validModels = def.models.map((m) => m.id);\n  const trimmed = (requested ?? '').trim();\n\n  // No model supplied → provider default.\n  if (trimmed.length === 0) {\n    const fallback = def.models.find((m) => m.id === def.defaultModel) ?? def.models[0];\n    return { ok: true, model: fallback };\n  }\n\n  const match = def.models.find((m) => m.id === trimmed);\n  if (match) {\n    return { ok: true, model: match };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    ok: false,\n    reason: `Model \"${trimmed}\" is not available for provider \"${provider}\"`,\n    validModels,\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Preflight USD/min for a (provider, model), including the medical add-on where\n * the provider meters it. Used only for the credit reservation; actual cost is\n * computed from the upstream response in the adapter.\n */\nexport function estimatedUsdPerMinute(\n  provider: SttProviderId,\n  model?: string,\n  medical: boolean = false,\n  keyterms: boolean = false,\n): number {\n  const resolution = resolveModel(provider, model);\n  const base = resolution.ok\n    ? resolution.model.estimatedUsdPerMinute\n    : PROVIDERS[provider].models[0].estimatedUsdPerMinute;\n\n  if (provider === 'elevenlabs') {\n    // Keyterm prompting adds +20% on base, scribe_v2 only (scribe_v1 has no biasing).\n    const resolvedModel = resolution.ok ? resolution.model.id : PROVIDERS.elevenlabs.models[0].id;\n    return (keyterms && resolvedModel === 'scribe_v2') ? base * (1 + ELEVENLABS_KEYTERMS_SURCHARGE) : base;\n  }\n\n  if (provider !== 'assemblyai') {\n    return base;\n  }\n\n  const resolvedModel = resolution.ok ? resolution.model.id : PROVIDERS.assemblyai.models[0].id;\n  const medicalAddon = medical ? (options.costEstimators?.length) {\n31\t    const maxEstimatedCost = Math.max(\n32\t      ...options.costEstimators.map((estimateCost) => estimateCost(estimatedSeconds))\n33\t    );\n34\t    return credits_remaining?: number; credits_deducted?: number };\n85\t    if (typeof data.credits_remaining === 'number') {\n86\t      await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n87\t        isValid: true,\n88\t Every\n103\t// deduction registers here so the SIGTERM handler can drain before exit.\n104\tconst inFlightDeductions = new Set<Promise<number>>();\n105\t\n106\texport async function drainPendingDeductions(timeoutMs: number): Promise<number> {\n107\t  const pendingCount = inFlightDeductions.size;\n108\t  if (pendingCount === 0) {\n109\t    return 0;\n110\t  }\n111\t\n112\t  const allSettled = Promise.allSettled([...inFlightDeductions]);\n113\t  const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));\n114\t  await Promise.race([allSettled, timeout]);\n115\t  return pendingCount;\n116\t}\n117\t\n118\texport function deductCredits(\n119\t  auth: AuthContext,\n120\t  costUsd: number,\n121\t  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n122\t  clientIP: string\n123\t): Promise<number> {\n124\t  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n125\t  inFlightDeductions.add(deduction);\n126\t  deduction\n127\t    .catch(() => {}) // errors are logged inside performDeduction / by callers\n128\t    .finally(() => inFlightDeductions.delete(deduction));\n129\t  return deduction;\n130\t}\n131\t\n132\tasync function performDeduction(\n133\t  auth: AuthContext,\n134\t  costUsd: number,\n135\t  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n136\t  _clientIP: string\n137\t): Promise<number> {\n138\t  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n139\t\n140\t  if (creditsUsed <= 0) {\n141\t    return 0;\n142\t  }\n143\t\n144\t  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\n145\t  return const balance = roundToTenth(auth.credits);\n  if (balance < estimatedCredits) {\n    return { ok: false, response: insufficientCreditsResponse(balance, estimatedCredits) };\n  }\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nasync function recordLicenseUsage(\n  licenseKey: string,\n  creditsUsed: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>\n): Promise<void> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/license/credits`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        amount: creditsUsed,\n        metadata,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      const errorData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      console.warn('POST network error', {\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// In-flight deduction tracking for graceful shutdown.\n// Call sites fire deductCredits() without awaiting (response latency), so a\n// Fly machine recycle (SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down) between the response\n// flush and the redis/license write would silently drop the charge. Every\n// deduction registers here so the SIGTERM handler can drain before exit.\nconst inFlightDeductions = new Set<Promise<number>>();\n\nexport async function drainPendingDeductions(timeoutMs: number): Promise<number> {\n  const pendingCount = inFlightDeductions.size;\n  if (pendingCount === 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  const allSettled = Promise.allSettled([...inFlightDeductions]);\n  const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));\n  await Promise.race([allSettled, timeout]);\n  return pendingCount;\n}\n\nexport function deductCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n  inFlightDeductions.add(deduction);\n  deduction\n    .catch(() => {}) // errors are logged inside performDeduction / by callers\n    .finally(() => inFlightDeductions.delete(deduction));\n  return deduction;\n}\n\nasync function performDeduction(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n  if (creditsUsed <= 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\n  return creditsUsed;\n}\n","numLines":147,"startLine":1,"totalLines":147}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9c347de1-75ae-4b89-ad26-b216ef007a63","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

The default model maps back to LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES so\n29\t// single-model providers (and the default of the multi-model ones) are\n30\t// unchanged; the non-default allowlisted models of the multi-model providers\n31\t// (openai/gemini/mistral) get their own label so the response reflects the model\n32\t// actually used instead of the provider default. MUST stay in sync with\n33\t// LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS allowlists.\n34\tconst LLM_SERVED_NAMES: Partial<Record<LLMProvider, Record<string, string>>> = {\n35\t  openai: { 'gpt-5-mini': 'openai-gpt-5-mini', 'gpt-5-nano': 'openai-gpt-5-nano' },\n36\t  gemini: { 'gemini-2.5-flash': 'gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite' },\n37\t  mistral: { 'mistral-small-latest': 'mistral-small-latest', 'open-mistral-nemo': 'open-mistral-nemo' },\n38\t};\n39\t\n40\t/**\n41\t * Served name for the X-LLM-Provider response header / log: the (provider,\n42\t * model) pair the request was actually answered with. For the single-model\n43\t * providers (and any model not in the served-name map) this is the static\n44\t * LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES label; for the multi-model providers' non-default models it\n45\t * echoes the resolved model so callers (and the smoke test) can tell a\n46\t * non-default model apart from the provider default.\n47\t */\n48\texport function servedLLMName(provider: LLMProvider, model: string): string {\n49\t  return LLM_SERVED_NAMES[provider]?.[model] ?? LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES[provider];\n50\t}\n51\t\n52\tconst LLM_PROVIDER_FALLBACKS: Record<LLMProvider, LLMProvider> = {\n53\t  anthropic: 'cerebras',\n54\t  cerebras: 'groq',\n55\t  groq: 'cerebras',\n56\t  grok: 'anthropic',\n57\t  openai: 'anthropic',\n58\t  gemini: 'cerebras',\n59\t  mistral: 'groq',\n60\t};\n61\t\n62\t// Per-provider allowlist of valid X-LLM-Model ids, with the default first. The\n63\t// resolved model is threaded through callWithRetry to the openai/gemini/mistral\n64\t// clients (the 4 single-model providers ignore it). MUST match the model ids in\n65\t// shared-app-classification/cloud-pp-catalog.json.\n66\tconst LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS: Record<LLMProvider, { default: string; allowed: readonly string[] }> = {\n67\t  cerebras: { default: 'gpt-oss-120b', allowed: ['gpt-oss-120b'] },\n68\t  groq: { default: 'openai/gpt-oss-120b', allowed: ['openai/gpt-oss-120b'] },\n69\t  anthropic: { default: 'claude-haiku-4-5', allowed: ['claude-haiku-4-5'] },\n70\t  grok: { default: 'grok-4.3', allowed: ['grok-4.3'] },\n71\t  openai: { default: 'gpt-5-mini', allowed: ['gpt-5-mini', 'gpt-5-nano'] },\n72\t  gemini: { default: 'gemini-2.5-flash', allowed: ['gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite'] },\n73\t  mistral: { default: 'mistral-small-latest', allowed: ['mistral-small-latest', 'open-mistral-nemo'] },\n74\t};\n75\t\n76\texport function defaultModelFor(provider: LLMProvider): string {\n77\t  return LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS[provider].default;\n78\t}\n79\t\n80\texport function fallbackProviderFor(provider: LLMProvider): LLMProvider {\n81\t  return LLM_PROVIDER_FALLBACKS[provider];\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\t/**\n85\t * Extract LLM provider from X-LLM-Provider header.\n86\t * Returns default provider if header is missing or invalid.\n87\t */\n88\texport function extractLLMProvider(request: Request): LLMProvider {\n89\t  const header = request.headers.get('x-llm-provider')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n90\t\n91\t  switch (header) {\n92\t    case 'groq':\n93\t    case 'cerebras':\n94\t    case 'anthropic':\n95\t    case 'grok':\n96\t    case 'openai':\n97\t    case 'gemini':\n98\t    case 'mistral':\n99\t      return header;\n100\t    default:\n101\t      return DEFAULT_LLM_PROVIDER;\n102\t  }\n103\t}\n104\t\n105\t/**\n106\t * Resolve the model for a provider from the X-LLM-Model header, validating it\n107\t * against the provider's allowlist. Missing or invalid models fall back to the\n108\t * provider default so a bad header never bills the wrong (or no) model.\n109\t */\n110\texport function resolveLLMModel(provider: LLMProvider, request: Request): string {\n111\t  const requested = request.headers.get('x-llm-model')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n112\t  const config = LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS[provider];\n113\t  if (requested && config.allowed.includes(requested)) {\n114\t    return requested;\n115\t  }\n116\t  return config.default;\n117\t}\n118\t\n119\t/**\n120\t * Retry LLM call with exponential backoff. `model` is the resolved (allowlisted)\n121\t * model id — the multi-model providers (openai/gemini/mistral) route on it; the\n122\t * single-model providers ignore it.\n123\t */\n124\texport async function callWithRetry(\n125\t  provider: LLMProvider,\n126\t  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n127\t  requestId: string,\n128\t  maxRetries: number,\n129\t  model: string\n130\t): Promise<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof requestCerebrasChat>>> {\n131\t  return retryWithBackoff(\n132\t    () => {\n133\t      if (provider === 'anthropic') return requestAnthropicChat(payload, requestId);\n134\t      if (provider === 'grok') return requestXaiGrokChat(payload, requestId);\n135\t      if (provider === 'openai') return requestOpenAIChat(payload, requestId, model);\n136\t      if (provider === 'gemini') return requestGeminiChat(payload, requestId, model);\n137\t      if (provider === 'mistral') return requestMistralChat(payload, requestId, model);\n138\t      return provider === 'cerebras'\n139\t        ? requestCerebrasChat(payload, requestId)\n140\t        : requestGroqChat(payload, requestId);\n141\t    },\n142\t    {\n143\t      maxRetries,\n144\t      initialDelayMs: 1000,\n145\t      backoffMultiplier: 2,\n146\t      onRetry: (attempt, error, delayMs) => {\n147\t        console.warn(`[llm] ${provider} failed - retrying`, {\n148\t          attempt,\n149\t          error: error.message,\n150\t          delayMs,\n151\t        });\n152\t      },\n153\t    }\n154\t  );\n155\t}\n156\t\n157\tfunction getErrorStatus(error: unknown): number | undefined {\n158\t  if (!error || typeof error !== 'object') {\n159\t    return undefined;\n160\t  }\n161\t\n162\t  const status = (error as { status?: unknown }).status;\n163\t  if (typeof status === LLM PROVIDER SELECTION + RETRY\n\nimport { retryWithBackoff } from './utils';\nimport type { CorrectionRequestPayload } from '../providers/groq-llm';\nimport { requestCerebrasChat } from '../providers/cerebras';\nimport { requestGroqChat } from '../providers/groq-llm';\nimport { requestAnthropicChat } from '../providers/anthropic';\nimport { requestXaiGrokChat } from '../providers/xai-llm';\nimport { requestOpenAIChat } from '../providers/openai-llm';\nimport { requestGeminiChat } from '../providers/gemini-llm';\nimport { requestMistralChat } from '../providers/mistral-llm';\n\nexport type LLMProvider = 'cerebras' | 'groq' | 'anthropic' | 'grok' | 'openai' | 'gemini' | 'mistral';\n\nexport const DEFAULT_LLM_PROVIDER: LLMProvider = 'cerebras';\n\nexport const LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES: Record<LLMProvider, string> = {\n  cerebras: 'cerebras-gpt-oss-120b',\n  groq: 'groq-gpt-oss-120b',\n  anthropic: 'claude-haiku-4-5',\n  grok: 'xai-grok-4.3',\n  openai: 'openai-gpt-5-mini',\n  gemini: 'gemini-2.5-flash',\n  mistral: 'mistral-small-latest',\n};\n\n// Served name per (provider, resolved-model) pair, for the X-LLM-Provider\n// response header / log. The default model maps back to LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES so\n// single-model providers (and the default of the multi-model ones) are\n// unchanged; the non-default allowlisted models of the multi-model providers\n// (openai/gemini/mistral) get their own label so the response reflects the model\n// actually used instead of the provider default. MUST stay in sync with\n// LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS allowlists.\nconst LLM_SERVED_NAMES: Partial<Record<LLMProvider, Record<string, string>>> = {\n  openai: { 'gpt-5-mini': 'openai-gpt-5-mini', 'gpt-5-nano': 'openai-gpt-5-nano' },\n  gemini: { 'gemini-2.5-flash': 'gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite': 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite' },\n  mistral: { 'mistral-small-latest': 'mistral-small-latest', 'open-mistral-nemo': 'open-mistral-nemo' },\n};\n\n/**\n * Served name for the X-LLM-Provider response header / log: the (provider,\n * model) pair the request was actually answered with. For the single-model\n * providers (and any model not in the served-name map) this is the static\n * LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES label; for the multi-model providers' non-default models it\n * echoes the resolved model so callers (and the smoke test) can tell a\n * non-default model apart from the provider default.\n */\nexport function servedLLMName(provider: LLMProvider, model: string): string {\n  return LLM_SERVED_NAMES[provider]?.[model] ?? LLM_PROVIDER_NAMES[provider];\n}\n\nconst LLM_PROVIDER_FALLBACKS: Record<LLMProvider, LLMProvider> = {\n  anthropic: 'cerebras',\n  cerebras: 'groq',\n  groq: 'cerebras',\n  grok: 'anthropic',\n  openai: 'anthropic',\n  gemini: 'cerebras',\n  mistral: 'groq',\n};\n\n// Per-provider allowlist of valid X-LLM-Model ids, with the default first. The\n// resolved model is threaded through callWithRetry to the openai/gemini/mistral\n// clients (the 4 single-model providers ignore it). MUST match the model ids in\n// shared-app-classification/cloud-pp-catalog.json.\nconst LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS: Record<LLMProvider, { default: string; allowed: readonly string[] }> = {\n  cerebras: { default: 'gpt-oss-120b', allowed: ['gpt-oss-120b'] },\n  groq: { default: 'openai/gpt-oss-120b', allowed: ['openai/gpt-oss-120b'] },\n  anthropic: { default: 'claude-haiku-4-5', allowed: ['claude-haiku-4-5'] },\n  grok: { default: 'grok-4.3', allowed: ['grok-4.3'] },\n  openai: { default: 'gpt-5-mini', allowed: ['gpt-5-mini', 'gpt-5-nano'] },\n  gemini: { default: 'gemini-2.5-flash', allowed: ['gemini-2.5-flash', 'gemini-2.5-flash-lite'] },\n  mistral: { default: 'mistral-small-latest', allowed: ['mistral-small-latest', 'open-mistral-nemo'] },\n};\n\nexport function defaultModelFor(provider: LLMProvider): string {\n  return LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS[provider].default;\n}\n\nexport function fallbackProviderFor(provider: LLMProvider): LLMProvider {\n  return LLM_PROVIDER_FALLBACKS[provider];\n}\n\n/**\n * Extract LLM provider from X-LLM-Provider header.\n * Returns default provider if header is missing or invalid.\n */\nexport function extractLLMProvider(request: Request): LLMProvider {\n  const header = request.headers.get('x-llm-provider')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n\n  switch (header) {\n    case 'groq':\n    case 'cerebras':\n    case 'anthropic':\n    case 'grok':\n    case 'openai':\n    case 'gemini':\n    case 'mistral':\n      return header;\n    default:\n      return DEFAULT_LLM_PROVIDER;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve the model for a provider from the X-LLM-Model header, validating it\n * against the provider's allowlist. Missing or invalid models fall back to the\n * provider default so a bad header never bills the wrong (or no) model.\n */\nexport function resolveLLMModel(provider: LLMProvider, request: Request): string {\n  const requested = request.headers.get('x-llm-model')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n  const config = LLM_PROVIDER_MODELS[provider];\n  if (requested && config.allowed.includes(requested)) {\n    return requested;\n  }\n  return config.default;\n}\n\n/**\n * Retry LLM call with exponential backoff. `model` is the resolved (allowlisted)\n * model id — the multi-model providers (openai/gemini/mistral) route on it; the\n * single-model providers ignore it.\n */\nexport async function callWithRetry(\n  provider: LLMProvider,\n  payload: CorrectionRequestPayload,\n  requestId: string,\n  maxRetries: number,\n  model: string\n): Promise<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof requestCerebrasChat>>> {\n  return retryWithBackoff(\n    () => {\n      if (provider === 'anthropic') return requestAnthropicChat(payload, requestId);\n      if (provider === 'grok') return requestXaiGrokChat(payload, requestId);\n      if (provider === 'openai') return requestOpenAIChat(payload, requestId, model);\n      if (provider === 'gemini') return requestGeminiChat(payload, requestId, model);\n      if (provider === 'mistral') return requestMistralChat(payload, requestId, model);\n      return provider === 'cerebras'\n        ? requestCerebrasChat(payload, requestId)\n        : requestGroqChat(payload, requestId);\n    },\n    {\n      maxRetries,\n      initialDelayMs: 1000,\n      backoffMultiplier: 2,\n      onRetry: (attempt, error, delayMs) => {\n        console.warn(`[llm] ${provider} failed - retrying`, {\n          attempt,\n          error: error.message,\n          delayMs,\n        });\n      },\n    }\n  );\n}\n\nfunction getErrorStatus(error: unknown): number | undefined {\n  if (!error || typeof error !== 'object') {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n\n  const status = (error as { status?: unknown }).status;\n  if (typeof status === 'number') {\n    return status;\n  }\n\n  const message = (error as { message?: unknown }).message;\n  if (typeof message === 'string') {\n    const match = message.match(/status\\s+(\\d{3})/i);\n    if (match) {\n      return Number(match[1]);\n    }\n  }\n\n  return undefined;\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if an error should trigger provider fallback (5xx).\n */\nexport function shouldFallback(error: unknown): boolean {\n  const status = getErrorStatus(error);\n  return typeof status === 'number' && status >= 500 && status <= 599;\n}\n","numLines":185,"startLine":1,"totalLines":185}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6229e86f-74d9-4671-930b-d7ab11eecbec","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

per-region smoke 2026-06-07).\n58\t// Requests landing here are replayed to `iad` before any work happens.\n59\tconst ELEVENLABS_BLOCKED_FLY_REGIONS = new Set(['nrt', 'bom', 'maa']);\n60\tconst ELEVENLABS_REPLAY_REGION = 'iad';\n61\t\n62\t// Human-readable base label per provider. The model is appended at runtime via\n63\t// formatProviderName() so the response header / metering reflects exactly which\n64\t// model ran (e.g. \"deepgram/nova-3-medical\", \"openai/gpt-4o-transcribe\").\n65\tconst PROVIDER_NAMES: Record<Provider, string> = {\n66\t  deepgram: 'deepgram',\n67\t  elevenlabs: 'elevenlabs',\n68\t  groq: 'groq',\n69\t  grok: 'xai-grok',\n70\t  'azure-mai': 'azure-mai',\n71\t  'google-chirp': 'google-chirp',\n72\t  openai: 'openai',\n73\t  gemini: 'gemini',\n74\t  assemblyai: 'assemblyai',\n75\t  mistral: 'mistral',\n76\t  soniox: 'soniox',\n77\t};\n78\t\n79\tfunction formatProviderName(provider: Provider, model: string): string {\n80\t  const base = PROVIDER_NAMES[provider];\n81\t  return model ? `${base}/${model}` : base;\n82\t}\n83\t\n84\t// Fallback chains: the original cheap trio (plus grok) cascade through\n85\t// alternatives — ElevenLabs (most expensive) is the last resort. Every other\n86\t// provider is SELF-ONLY: the caller picked that specific model, so on failure\n87\t// we surface an error rather than silently substituting a different model and\n88\t// price. (A cross-provider fallback would also change the metered cost.)\n89\tconst FALLBACK_CHAINS: Record<Provider, Provider[]> = {\n90\t  elevenlabs: ['elevenlabs', 'deepgram', 'groq'],\n91\t  groq: ['groq', 'deepgram', 'elevenlabs'],\n92\t  deepgram: ['deepgram', 'groq', 'elevenlabs'],\n93\t  grok: ['grok', 'deepgram', 'groq', 'elevenlabs'],\n94\t  'azure-mai': ['azure-mai'],\n95\t  'google-chirp': ['google-chirp'],\n96\t  openai: ['openai'],\n97\t  gemini: ['gemini'],\n98\t  assemblyai: ['assemblyai'],\n99\t  mistral: ['mistral'],\n100\t  soniox: ['soniox'],\n101\t};\n102\t\n103\tconst PROVIDER_FN: Record<Provider, (\n104\t  audio: ArrayBuffer,\n105\t  contentType: string,\n106\t  language?: string,\n107\t  initialPrompt?: string,\n108\t  context?: ProviderRequestContext,\n109\t) => Promise<TranscriptionResult>> = {\n110\t  deepgram: transcribeWithDeepgram,\n111\t  groq: transcribeWithGroq,\n112\t  elevenlabs: transcribeWithElevenLabs,\n113\t  grok: transcribeWithXaiGrok,\n114\t  'azure-mai': transcribeWithAzureMai,\n115\t  'google-chirp': transcribeWithGoogleChirp,\n116\t  openai: transcribeWithOpenAI,\n117\t  gemini: transcribeWithGemini,\n118\t  assemblyai: transcribeWithAssemblyAI,\n119\t  mistral: transcribeWithMistral,\n120\t  soniox: transcribeWithSoniox,\n121\t};\n122\t\n123\t/**\n124\t * Preflight credit reservation. For the primary provider we estimate against\n125\t * the chosen model (and medical add-on); for fallback siblings we estimate\n126\t * against their default model. The reservation uses the most expensive member\n127\t * of the chain so we never under-reserve. `model`/`medical` are optional to\n128\t * keep the historical 2-arg call signature working.\n129\t */\n130\texport function estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks(\n131\t  sizeBytes: number,\n132\t  provider: Provider,\n133\t  model?: string,\n134\t  medical: boolean = false,\n135\t  initialPrompt?: string,\n136\t  language?: string,\n137\t): number {\n138\t  const chain = FALLBACK_CHAINS[provider];\n139\t  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n140\t  const hasInitialPrompt = Boolean(initialPrompt);\n141\t  const rates = chain.map((p) => estimatedUsdPerMinute(\n142\t    p,\n143\t    p === provider ? model : undefined,\n144\t    p === provider ? medical : false,\n145\t    // The keyterm surcharge is billed by ANY chain member that supports it\n146\t    // (ElevenLabs scribe_v2 / AssemblyAI universal-3-pro) whenever an\n147\t    // initial_prompt is present — not just the primary provider. A\n148\t    // Deepgram→ElevenLabs fallback still forwards initial_prompt and bills the\n149\t    // +20% surcharge, so reserve for it on every eligible sibling. Other\n150\t    // providers ignore the flag (estimatedUsdPerMinute scopes the add-on), so\n151\t    // this never over-reserves for, say, a Deepgram-only success path.\n152\t    hasInitialPrompt && (p === 'elevenlabs' || p === 'assemblyai'),\n153\t  ));\n154\t  // AssemblyAI's sync fast path (<120s, non-medical, EXPLICIT-language clips)\n155\t  // always runs universal-3-5-pro at its OWN higher published rate — not the\n156\t  // async catalog rate for the requested model (universal-2 / universal-3-pro),\n157\t  // which `estimatedUsdPerMinute` above reserves against. A short clip is\n158\t  // exactly sync's target case, so without this a short, non-medical\n159\t  // AssemblyAI request could be deducted for more than was reserved. This\n160\t  // condition must exactly mirror `transcribeWithAssemblyAI`'s real\n161\t  // eligibility gate (medical + duration + explicit language) — reusing\n162\t  // `hasExplicitLanguage` rather than reimplementing it here, since an\n163\t  // auto-language request never actually routes through sync (sync has no\n164\t  // auto-detect) and over-reserving for it could wrongly reject a low-balance\n165\t  // user at preflight for a request that will only ever go through the\n166\t  // cheaper async path.\n167\t  if (\n168\t    provider === 'assemblyai' && !medical\n169\t    && estimatedSeconds < ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ELIGIBLE_ESTIMATED_SECONDS\n170\t    && hasExplicitAssemblyAILanguage(language)\n171\t  ) {\n172\t    rates.push(ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE);\n173\t  }\n174\t  const usdPerMinute = Math.max(...rates);\n175\t  // Token-billed providers (Gemini, OpenAI gpt-4o*) charge the prompt text as\n176\t  // input tokens on top of the audio. Reserve that flat cost for the primary\n177\t  // provider (these are self-only chains) so a large vocabulary prompt on a\n178\t  // short clip can't be deducted beyond what was reserved.\n179\t  const promptReservationUsd = estimatePromptInputReservationUsd(provider, model, initialPrompt);\n180\t  const estimatedCostUsd = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * usdPerMinute + promptReservationUsd;\n181\t  return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(estimatedCostUsd));\n182\t}\n183\t\n184\ttype ProviderSelection =\n185\t  | { ok: true; provider: Provider }\n186\t  | { ok: false; provided: string };\n187\t\n188\tfunction extractProvider(c: Context): ProviderSelection {\n189\t  const header = c.req.header('X-STT-Provider')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n190\t  // No header → historical default (many clients send only a provider, some\n191\t  // none). An explicitly-supplied but unknown provider is REJECTED (fail-closed)\n192\t  // rather than silently billed against a default upstream.\n193\t  if (!header) {\n194\t    return { ok: true, provider: 'deepgram' };\n195\t  }\n196\t  if (isValidProviderId(header)) {\n197\t    return { ok: true, provider: header };\n198\t  }\n199\t  return { ok: false, provided: header };\n200\t}\n201\t\n202\tfunction extractModel(c: Context): string | undefined {\n203\t  return c.req.header('X-STT-Model')?.trim() || rawQuery(c.req.url, 'model')?.trim() || undefined;\n204\t}\n205\t\n206\tfunction extractDomain(c: Context): string | undefined {\n207\t  const domain = c.req.header('X-STT-Domain')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n208\t  return domain || undefined;\n209\t}\n210\t\n211\tfunction validateStreamingHeaders(c: Context, provider: Provider):\n212\t  | { ok: true; contentType: string; contentLength: number }\n213\t  | { ok: false; response: Response } {\n214\t  const contentType = c.req.header('Content-Type') || '';\n215\t  if (!contentType.startsWith('audio/')) {\n216\t    return { ok: false, response: invalidContentTypeResponse('audio/*', contentType) };\n217\t  }\n218\t\n219\t  const contentLengthHeader = c.req.header('Content-Length');\n220\t  if (!contentLengthHeader) {\n221\t    return { ok: false, response: missingContentLengthResponse() };\n222\t  }\n223\t\n224\t  const contentLength = Number.parseInt(contentLengthHeader, 10);\n225\t  if (!Number.isFinite(contentLength) || contentLength <= 0) {\n226\t    return { ok: false, response: errorResponse(400, 'Invalid Content-Length', 'Content-Length must be a positive integer') };\n227\t  }\n228\t\n229\t  if (contentLength > MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES) {\n230\t    return { ok: false, response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES) };\n231\t  }\n232\t\n233\t  // Google Chirp inline cap (~9.5 MB) applies before we buffer the body —\n234\t  // without a scratch GCS bucket the provider has no path for larger audio,\n235\t  // and we don't want to allocate a 50 MB buffer just to 413 the caller.\n236\t  if (\n237\t    provider === 'google-chirp'\n238\t    && contentLength > GOOGLE_CHIRP_INLINE_MAX_BYTES\n239\t    && !(process.env.GOOGLE_SPEECH_GCS_BUCKET || '').trim()\n240\t  ) {\n241\t    return {\n242\t      ok: false,\n243\t      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, GOOGLE_CHIRP_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n244\t    };\n245\t  }\n246\t\n247\t  // Gemini sends audio inline (base64) and rejects anything over ~14 MB raw.\n248\t  // Gate on Content-Length before buffering so an oversized upload is rejected\n249\t  // early instead of after buffering up to MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES on the machine.\n250\t  if (provider === 'gemini' && contentLength > GEMINI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES) {\n251\t    return {\n252\t      ok: false,\n253\t      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, GEMINI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n254\t    };\n255\t  }\n256\t\n257\t  // OpenAI hard-rejects audio over 25 MB with a 400. Gate on Content-Length\n258\t  // before buffering so we return 413 without allocating the buffer first.\n259\t  if (provider === 'openai' && contentLength > OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES) {\n260\t    return {\n261\t      ok: false,\n262\t      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n263\t    };\n264\t  }\n265\t\n266\t  return { ok: true, contentType, contentLength };\n267\t}\n268\t\n269\texport async function transcribeRoute(c: Context) {\n270\t  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n271\t  const startTime = performance.now();\n272\t  const clientIP = 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n278\t      reason: 'ip_blocked',\n279\t      flyRequestId,\n280\t    });\n281\t    return errorResponse(403, 'Access denied', 'Your IP has been temporarily blocked due to abuse');\n282\t  }\n283\t  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.ip_check_done', { flyRequestId });\n284\t\n285\t  const providerSelection = extractProvider(c);\n286\t  if (!providerSelection.ok) {\n287\t    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n288\t      reason: 'invalid_provider',\n289\t      flyRequestId,\n290\t      provided: providerSelection.provided,\n291\t    });\n292\t    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid STT provider',\n293\t      `Unknown X-STT-Provider \"${providerSelection.provided}\".`,\n294\t      { requestId, provided: providerSelection.provided },\n295\t    );\n296\t  }\n297\t  const provider = providerSelection.provider;\n298\t\n299\t  // Resolve + validate the requested model against the server-side registry.\n300\t  // An unknown model for the provider is rejected (fail-closed) rather '../lib/logging';\n\n// Supported providers (mirror the server-side registry in lib/stt-models.ts).\nexport type Provider = SttProviderId;\n\n// Fly regions where ElevenLabs serves a text/html FAQ page instead of JSON\n// (geo-block on Japan + India confirmed via per-region smoke 2026-06-07).\n// Requests landing here are replayed to `iad` before any work happens.\nconst ELEVENLABS_BLOCKED_FLY_REGIONS = new Set(['nrt', 'bom', 'maa']);\nconst ELEVENLABS_REPLAY_REGION = 'iad';\n\n// Human-readable base label per provider. The model is appended at runtime via\n// formatProviderName() so the response header / metering reflects exactly which\n// model ran (e.g. \"deepgram/nova-3-medical\", \"openai/gpt-4o-transcribe\").\nconst PROVIDER_NAMES: Record<Provider, string> = {\n  deepgram: 'deepgram',\n  elevenlabs: 'elevenlabs',\n  groq: 'groq',\n  grok: 'xai-grok',\n  'azure-mai': 'azure-mai',\n  'google-chirp': 'google-chirp',\n  openai: 'openai',\n  gemini: 'gemini',\n  assemblyai: 'assemblyai',\n  mistral: 'mistral',\n  soniox: 'soniox',\n};\n\nfunction formatProviderName(provider: Provider, model: string): string {\n  const base = PROVIDER_NAMES[provider];\n  return model ? `${base}/${model}` : base;\n}\n\n// Fallback chains: the original cheap trio (plus grok) cascade through\n// alternatives — ElevenLabs (most expensive) is the last resort. Every other\n// provider is SELF-ONLY: the caller picked that specific model, so on failure\n// we surface an error rather than silently substituting a different model and\n// price. (A cross-provider fallback would also change the metered cost.)\nconst FALLBACK_CHAINS: Record<Provider, Provider[]> = {\n  elevenlabs: ['elevenlabs', 'deepgram', 'groq'],\n  groq: ['groq', 'deepgram', 'elevenlabs'],\n  deepgram: ['deepgram', 'groq', 'elevenlabs'],\n  grok: ['grok', 'deepgram', 'groq', 'elevenlabs'],\n  'azure-mai': ['azure-mai'],\n  'google-chirp': ['google-chirp'],\n  openai: ['openai'],\n  gemini: ['gemini'],\n  assemblyai: ['assemblyai'],\n  mistral: ['mistral'],\n  soniox: ['soniox'],\n};\n\nconst PROVIDER_FN: Record<Provider, (\n  audio: ArrayBuffer,\n  contentType: string,\n  language?: string,\n  initialPrompt?: string,\n  context?: ProviderRequestContext,\n) => Promise<TranscriptionResult>> = {\n  deepgram: transcribeWithDeepgram,\n  groq: transcribeWithGroq,\n  elevenlabs: transcribeWithElevenLabs,\n  grok: transcribeWithXaiGrok,\n  'azure-mai': transcribeWithAzureMai,\n  'google-chirp': transcribeWithGoogleChirp,\n  openai: transcribeWithOpenAI,\n  gemini: transcribeWithGemini,\n  assemblyai: transcribeWithAssemblyAI,\n  mistral: transcribeWithMistral,\n  soniox: transcribeWithSoniox,\n};\n\n/**\n * Preflight credit reservation. For the primary provider we estimate against\n * the chosen model (and medical add-on); for fallback siblings we estimate\n * against their default model. The reservation uses the most expensive member\n * of the chain so we never under-reserve. `model`/`medical` are optional to\n * keep the historical 2-arg call signature working.\n */\nexport function estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks(\n  sizeBytes: number,\n  provider: Provider,\n  model?: string,\n  medical: boolean = false,\n  initialPrompt?: string,\n  language?: string,\n): number {\n  const chain = FALLBACK_CHAINS[provider];\n  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n  const hasInitialPrompt = Boolean(initialPrompt);\n  const rates = chain.map((p) => estimatedUsdPerMinute(\n    p,\n    p === provider ? model : undefined,\n    p === provider ? medical : false,\n    // The keyterm surcharge is billed by ANY chain member that supports it\n    // (ElevenLabs scribe_v2 / AssemblyAI universal-3-pro) whenever an\n    // initial_prompt is present — not just the primary provider. A\n    // Deepgram→ElevenLabs fallback still forwards initial_prompt and bills the\n    // +20% surcharge, so reserve for it on every eligible sibling. Other\n    // providers ignore the flag (estimatedUsdPerMinute scopes the add-on), so\n    // this never over-reserves for, say, a Deepgram-only success path.\n    hasInitialPrompt && (p === 'elevenlabs' || p === 'assemblyai'),\n  ));\n  // AssemblyAI's sync fast path (<120s, non-medical, EXPLICIT-language clips)\n  // always runs universal-3-5-pro at its OWN higher published rate — not the\n  // async catalog rate for the requested model (universal-2 / universal-3-pro),\n  // which `estimatedUsdPerMinute` above reserves against. A short clip is\n  // exactly sync's target case, so without this a short, non-medical\n  // AssemblyAI request could be deducted for more than was reserved. This\n  // condition must exactly mirror `transcribeWithAssemblyAI`'s real\n  // eligibility gate (medical + duration + explicit language) — reusing\n  // `hasExplicitLanguage` rather than reimplementing it here, since an\n  // auto-language request never actually routes through sync (sync has no\n  // auto-detect) and over-reserving for it could wrongly reject a low-balance\n  // user at preflight for a request that will only ever go through the\n  // cheaper async path.\n  if (\n    provider === 'assemblyai' && !medical\n    && estimatedSeconds < ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ELIGIBLE_ESTIMATED_SECONDS\n    && hasExplicitAssemblyAILanguage(language)\n  ) {\n    rates.push(ASSEMBLYAI_SYNC_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_MINUTE);\n  }\n  const usdPerMinute = Math.max(...rates);\n  // Token-billed providers (Gemini, OpenAI gpt-4o*) charge the prompt text as\n  // input tokens on top of the audio. Reserve that flat cost for the primary\n  // provider (these are self-only chains) so a large vocabulary prompt on a\n  // short clip can't be deducted beyond what was reserved.\n  const promptReservationUsd = estimatePromptInputReservationUsd(provider, model, initialPrompt);\n  const estimatedCostUsd = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * usdPerMinute + promptReservationUsd;\n  return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(estimatedCostUsd));\n}\n\ntype ProviderSelection =\n  | { ok: true; provider: Provider }\n  | { ok: false; provided: string };\n\nfunction extractProvider(c: Context): ProviderSelection {\n  const header = c.req.header('X-STT-Provider')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n  // No header → historical default (many clients send only a provider, some\n  // none). An explicitly-supplied but unknown provider is REJECTED (fail-closed)\n  // rather than silently billed against a default upstream.\n  if (!header) {\n    return { ok: true, provider: 'deepgram' };\n  }\n  if (isValidProviderId(header)) {\n    return { ok: true, provider: header };\n  }\n  return { ok: false, provided: header };\n}\n\nfunction extractModel(c: Context): string | undefined {\n  return c.req.header('X-STT-Model')?.trim() || rawQuery(c.req.url, 'model')?.trim() || undefined;\n}\n\nfunction extractDomain(c: Context): string | undefined {\n  const domain = c.req.header('X-STT-Domain')?.toLowerCase().trim();\n  return domain || undefined;\n}\n\nfunction validateStreamingHeaders(c: Context, provider: Provider):\n  | { ok: true; contentType: string; contentLength: number }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response } {\n  const contentType = c.req.header('Content-Type') || '';\n  if (!contentType.startsWith('audio/')) {\n    return { ok: false, response: invalidContentTypeResponse('audio/*', contentType) };\n  }\n\n  const contentLengthHeader = c.req.header('Content-Length');\n  if (!contentLengthHeader) {\n    return { ok: false, response: missingContentLengthResponse() };\n  }\n\n  const contentLength = Number.parseInt(contentLengthHeader, 10);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(contentLength) || contentLength <= 0) {\n    return { ok: false, response: errorResponse(400, 'Invalid Content-Length', 'Content-Length must be a positive integer') };\n  }\n\n  if (contentLength > MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES) {\n    return { ok: false, response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES) };\n  }\n\n  // Google Chirp inline cap (~9.5 MB) applies before we buffer the body —\n  // without a scratch GCS bucket the provider has no path for larger audio,\n  // and we don't want to allocate a 50 MB buffer just to 413 the caller.\n  if (\n    provider === 'google-chirp'\n    && contentLength > GOOGLE_CHIRP_INLINE_MAX_BYTES\n    && !(process.env.GOOGLE_SPEECH_GCS_BUCKET || '').trim()\n  ) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, GOOGLE_CHIRP_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n    };\n  }\n\n  // Gemini sends audio inline (base64) and rejects anything over ~14 MB raw.\n  // Gate on Content-Length before buffering so an oversized upload is rejected\n  // early instead of after buffering up to MAX_AUDIO_SIZE_BYTES on the machine.\n  if (provider === 'gemini' && contentLength > GEMINI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, GEMINI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n    };\n  }\n\n  // OpenAI hard-rejects audio over 25 MB with a 400. Gate on Content-Length\n  // before buffering so we return 413 without allocating the buffer first.\n  if (provider === 'openai' && contentLength > OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      response: fileTooLargeResponse(contentLength, OPENAI_INLINE_MAX_BYTES),\n    };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true, contentType, contentLength };\n}\n\nexport async function transcribeRoute(c: Context) {\n  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n  const startTime = performance.now();\n  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n  const flyRequestId = getFlyRequestId(c);\n\n  // IP block check\n  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'ip_blocked',\n      flyRequestId,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(403, 'Access denied', 'Your IP has been temporarily blocked due to abuse');\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.ip_check_done', { flyRequestId });\n\n  const providerSelection = extractProvider(c);\n  if (!providerSelection.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'invalid_provider',\n      flyRequestId,\n      provided: providerSelection.provided,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid STT provider',\n      `Unknown X-STT-Provider \"${providerSelection.provided}\".`,\n      { requestId, provided: providerSelection.provided },\n    );\n  }\n  const provider = providerSelection.provider;\n\n  // Resolve + validate the requested model against the server-side registry.\n  // An unknown model for the provider is rejected (fail-closed) rather than","numLines":300,"startLine":1,"totalLines":735}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ecf7f012-bcf7-4922-aaef-9cc8f6c1f042","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Bill it at the text-input rate — not the audio rate,\n  // and not $0. audio + text always sums to promptTokenCount, so there's no\n  // double-count and no unbilled remainder. at a possibly different price.\n  const requestedModel = extractModel(c);\n  const modelResolution = resolveModel(provider, requestedModel);\n  if (!modelResolution.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'invalid_model',\n      flyRequestId,\n      provider,\n      requestedModel,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid STT model', modelResolution.reason, {\n      requestId,\n      provider,\n      requested_model: requestedModel,\n      valid_models: modelResolution.validModels,\n    });\n  }\n  const model = modelResolution.model.id;\n\n  const domain = extractDomain(c);\n  // Medical add-on only applies where the provider meters it (AssemblyAI today).\n  const medical = domain === MEDICAL_DOMAIN;\n\n  const headerValidation = validateStreamingHeaders(c, provider);\n  if (!headerValidation.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'invalid_streaming_headers',\n      flyRequestId,\n      provider,\n      status: headerValidation.response.status,\n    });\n    return headerValidation.response;\n  }\n\n  const { contentType, contentLength } = headerValidation;\n  // rawQuery, not c.req.query(): Hono's decoder adds an HTML-form `+` → space\n  // step, corrupting values like a `C++` vocabulary term. See lib/query.ts.\n  const language = rawQuery(c.req.url, 'language');\n  const initialPrompt = rawQuery(c.req.url, 'initial_prompt');\n  const mode = rawQuery(c.req.url, 'mode');\n\n  // ElevenLabs blocks API access from certain countries — the block surfaces\n  // as a 200 OK with a text/html FAQ page (\"Do you restrict access ... for any\n  // specific countries?\") instead of JSON. When the request lands on a Fly\n  // machine in one of those countries, replay it via Fly's edge to `iad`\n  // before doing any auth/credit work. Adds ~50-80ms vs ~6s of failure.\n  // Verified blocked regions (2026-06-07): nrt (JP), bom (IN), maa (IN).\n  //\n  // Fly only honours `fly-replay` for request bodies ≤ 1 MB; larger requests\n  // are silently executed in the original region. For oversized uploads from\n  // a blocked region we skip the replay header and let the chain fall back\n  // to the next provider instead of letting ElevenLabs return its HTML 200.\n  let elevenlabsGeoBlocked = false;\n  if (provider === 'elevenlabs' && ELEVENLABS_BLOCKED_FLY_REGIONS.has(process.env.FLY_REGION || '')) {\n    if (contentLength <= FLY_REPLAY_MAX_BODY_BYTES) {\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.fly_replay', {\n        flyRequestId,\n        provider,\n        fromRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION,\n        toRegion: ELEVENLABS_REPLAY_REGION,\n        reason: 'elevenlabs_geo_block',\n      });\n      c.header('fly-replay', `region=${ELEVENLABS_REPLAY_REGION}`);\n      return c.body(null, 200);\n    }\n\n    elevenlabsGeoBlocked = true;\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.fly_replay_skipped_oversized', {\n      flyRequestId,\n      provider,\n      flyRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION,\n      contentLength,\n      replayMaxBytes: FLY_REPLAY_MAX_BODY_BYTES,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const proxyOverheadMs = flyProxyOverheadMs(c.req.header('Fly-Request-Start'));\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_start', {\n    flyRequestId,\n    flyRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n    flyMachineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID,\n    proxyOverheadMs,\n    provider,\n    model: model || 'default',\n    domain: domain || 'none',\n    contentType,\n    contentLength,\n    language: language || 'auto',\n    hasInitialPrompt: Boolean(initialPrompt),\n    mode: mode || 'default',\n  });\n\n  // Auth (query params only) — Cloud is licensed-only; a valid account key is required.\n  // `account_key` is the canonical param name; `license_key` is the legacy alias\n  // that installed native apps still send, so we accept either.\n  const authResult = await validateAuth({\n    licenseKey:\n      rawQuery(c.req.url, 'account_key') ?? rawQuery(c.req.url, 'license_key'),\n  });\n  if (!authResult.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'auth_failed',\n      flyRequestId,\n      status: authResult.response.status,\n    });\n    return authResult.response;\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.auth_done');\n\n  // Vocabulary surcharge: AssemblyAI charges a keyterms_prompt add-on (universal-3-pro)\n  // and ElevenLabs a +20% keyterm surcharge (scribe_v2) when an initial_prompt is supplied.\n  // We pass the raw hasInitialPrompt flag through to the reservation so it can reserve the\n  // surcharge for ANY eligible chain member — including ElevenLabs reached via a\n  // Deepgram/Groq/Grok fallback, which still forwards the prompt and bills the surcharge.\n  // estimatedUsdPerMinute scopes the add-on to universal-3-pro / scribe_v2, so passing it\n  // for every request is safe and never under-reserves.\n  const estimatedCredits = estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks(contentLength, provider, model, medical, initialPrompt, language);\n  const creditCheck = await validateCredits(authResult.value, estimatedCredits, clientIP);\n  if (!creditCheck.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'credits_failed',\n      flyRequestId,\n      status: creditCheck.response.status,\n      estimatedCredits,\n    });\n    return creditCheck.response;\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.credits_done', { estimatedCredits });","numLines":130,"startLine":299,"totalLines":735}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b2e66561-f9c5-453f-b438-ec61ba97c670","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{ NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { validateCreditDeductionAmount } from \"./validation\";\n\nimport {\n  deductCreditBalance,\n  findAccountByKey,\n  getCreditBalance,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\n/**\n * License Credits API\n *\n * Manages credit balance for licensed users.\n * Used by HyperWhisper Cloud (CF Workers) to check balance and deduct credits.\n *\n * ENDPOINTS:\n * - GET: Get credit balance for a license key\n * - POST: Deduct credits (record usage)\n *\n * SECURITY:\n * - The license key itself acts as authentication\n * - Only valid license keys can query/deduct credits\n */\n\n/**\n * GET /api/license/credits\n *\n * Get credit balance for a license key.\n */\nexport async function GET(req: NextRequest) {\n  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);\n  const licenseKey = searchParams.get(\"license_key\");\n\n  if (!licenseKey) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"license_key is required\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey.trim());\n\n    if (!license) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"License key not found\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const credits = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n\n    return NextResponse.json({\n      credits,\n      stripe_customer_id: license.stripeCustomerId,\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credits balance error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Failed to get credit balance\" },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * POST /api/license/credits\n *\n * Deduct credits from a license (record usage).\n */\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { license_key, amount, metadata } = body;\n\n    if (!license_key || typeof license_key !== \"string\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"license_key is required\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const amountError = validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount);\n\n    if (amountError) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const license = await findAccountByKey(license_key.trim());\n\n    if (!license) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"License key not found\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    let newCredits: number;\n\n    try {\n      // Atomic SQL decrement (floored at 0) — concurrent POSTs cannot\n      // double-spend via a read-then-write race.\n      newCredits = await deductCreditBalance(license.userId, amount);\n    } catch (updateError) {\n      console.error(\"Credit deduction failed:\", updateError);\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"Failed to deduct credits. Please retry.\" },\n        { status: 409 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    console.log(`Credits deducted: ${amount} from license ${license_key.substring(0, 7)}...`, {\n      remaining: newCredits,\n      metadata,\n    });\n\n    return NextResponse.json({\n      credits_remaining: newCredits,\n      credits_deducted: amount,\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credits deduction error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"9d1f02be-a480-416a-a595-0594e4acc95c","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:41:31.990Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"import { {"parentUuid":"b1780689-9f44-4eab-8c7a-cca1c7a6eaf8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"a18f2b8332658cbb6","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015NDgFhr3ubKjt9wWhCs1CH","type":"tool_result","content":"export const MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT = 1_000_000;\n\n// credit_balances.balance is numeric(_, 2); amounts with finer precision than\n// the column scale would be silently rounded by Postgres on write, so a\n// sub-cent deduction (e.g. 0.005) can net a zero deduction. Reject anything\n// that does not round-trip at 2 decimal places.\nexport const CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE = 2;\n\nexport function validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount: unknown): string | null {\n  if (typeof amount !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {\n    return \"amount must be a finite positive number\";\n  }\n\n  if (amount > MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT) {\n    return `amount must be ${MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT} or less`;\n  }\n\n  if (Number(amount.toFixed(CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE)) !== amount) {\n    return `amount must have at most ${CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE} decimal places`;\n  }\n\n  return null;\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"5ea240bc-5596-43f3-bda0-2da89e513cad","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:41:40.105Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"export const MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT = 1_000_000;\n\n// credit_balances.balance is numeric(_, 2); amounts with finer precision than\n// the column scale would be silently rounded by Postgres on write, so a\n// sub-cent deduction (e.g. 0.005) can net a zero deduction. Reject anything\n// that does not round-trip at 2 decimal places.\nexport const CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE = 2;\n\nexport function validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount: unknown): string | null {\n  if (typeof amount !== \"number\" || !Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {\n    return \"amount must be a finite positive number\";\n  }\n\n  if (amount > MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT) {\n    return `amount must be ${MAX_CREDIT_DEDUCTION_AMOUNT} or less`;\n  }\n\n  if (Number(amount.toFixed(CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE)) !== amount) {\n    return `amount must have at most ${CREDIT_BALANCE_SCALE} decimal places`;\n  }\n\n  return null;\n}","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b1780689-9f44-4eab-8c7a-cca1c7a6eaf8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"b5bdd636-fa62-40e0-81ae-23801f73b4aa","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a18f2b8332658cbb6","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWHeeRxRtk18YneaPCBY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DQZkU3ZCxSMGi96rCKMPYL","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-ray-Desktop-ray-os/0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c/scratchpad/oracle-hunter/repro/s1-money-lookup.test.ts","content":"// [S1] probe: are the unchecked index-signature lookups in MONEY arithmetic\n// (cost-calculator.ts 303-316/340/437-438, stt-models.ts 243/272/276/284)\n// reachable with a key that MISSES, and if so does NaN escape into billing?\nimport { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';\nimport {\n  computeGeminiChatCost,\n  computeOpenAIChatCost,\n  computeMistralChatCost,\n  computeGeminiTranscriptionCost,\n  estimatePromptInputReservationUsd,\n  creditsForCost,\n  estimateCreditsForCost,\n} from '/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/cost-calculator';\nimport {\n  isValidProviderId,\n  resolveModel,\n  estimatedUsdPerMinute,\n} from '/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/stt-models';\nimport { resolveLLMModel, extractLLMProvider } from '/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/llm-provider';\n\nconst usage = { prompt_tokens: 1_000_000, completion_tokens: 1_000_000, total_tokens: 2_000_000 };\n// Keys that defeat a `map[key] ?? DEFAULT` guard: they resolve THROUGH the\n// prototype chain to a truthy non-rate object, so `??` never fires.\nconst PROTO_KEYS = ['__proto__', 'constructor', 'toString', 'valueOf', 'hasOwnProperty'];\n\ndescribe('A. is the internal lookup NaN-able at all?', () => {\n  test('plain unknown model -> `?? default` fires, finite price', () => {\n    console.log('computeOpenAIChatCost(\"nonexistent-model\") =', computeOpenAIChatCost('nonexistent-model', usage));\n    console.log('computeGeminiChatCost(\"nonexistent-model\") =', computeGeminiChatCost('nonexistent-model', usage));\n    console.log('computeMistralChatCost(\"nonexistent-model\") =', computeMistralChatCost('nonexistent-model', usage));\n    expect(Number.isFinite(computeOpenAIChatCost('nonexistent-model', usage))).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  test('prototype-chain key DEFEATS the `??` default -> NaN (internal call only)', () => {\n    for (const k of PROTO_KEYS) {\n      const openai = computeOpenAIChatCost(k, usage);\n      const gemini = computeGeminiChatCost(k, usage);\n      const geminiStt = computeGeminiTranscriptionCost(k, { audioInputTokens: 1920, outputTokens: 100 });\n      const reservation = estimatePromptInputReservationUsd('gemini', k, 'x'.repeat(400));\n      console.log(\n        `key=${JSON.stringify(k).padEnd(16)} openaiChat=${openai} geminiChat=${gemini} geminiStt=${geminiStt} reservationUsd=${reservation}`\n      );\n    }\n    expect(Number.isNaN(computeOpenAIChatCost('__proto__', usage))).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('B. can a client-supplied field actually deliver that key?', () => {\n  test('POST /transcribe  X-STT-Provider -> isValidProviderId rejects prototype keys', () => {\n    for (const k of [...PROTO_KEYS, 'nonexistent']) {\n      console.log(`isValidProviderId(${JSON.stringify(k)}) =`, isValidProviderId(k));\n      expect(isValidProviderId(k)).toBe(false);\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('POST /transcribe  X-STT-Model / ?model= -> resolveModel rejects prototype keys (400)', () => {\n    for (const k of [...PROTO_KEYS, 'nonexistent']) {\n      const r = resolveModel('gemini', k);\n      console.log(`resolveModel(\"gemini\", ${JSON.stringify(k)}) =>`, JSON.stringify(r));\n      expect(r.ok).toBe(false);\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('estimatedUsdPerMinute is never reached with an unresolved model, and is finite anyway', () => {\n    for (const k of [...PROTO_KEYS, 'nonexistent']) {\n      const v = estimatedUsdPerMinute('gemini', k);\n      console.log(`estimatedUsdPerMinute(\"gemini\", ${JSON.stringify(k)}) =`, v);\n      expect(Number.isFinite(v)).toBe(true);\n      expect(v).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('POST /post-process  X-LLM-Model -> resolveLLMModel allowlists, prototype keys collapse to default', () => {\n    for (const k of [...PROTO_KEYS, 'nonexistent']) {\n      const req = new Request('https://x/post-process', { headers: { 'x-llm-provider': 'openai', 'x-llm-model': k } });\n      const provider = extractLLMProvider(req);\n      const model = resolveLLMModel(provider, req);\n      console.log(`x-llm-model: ${JSON.stringify(k).padEnd(16)} -> provider=${provider} resolvedModel=${model} cost=${computeOpenAIChatCost(model, usage)}`);\n      expect(model).toBe('gpt-5-mini');\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('X-LLM-Provider prototype key -> extractLLMProvider switch falls to default', () => {\n    for (const k of PROTO_KEYS) {\n      const req = new Request('https://x/post-process', { headers: { 'x-llm-provider': k } });\n      console.log(`x-llm-provider: ${JSON.stringify(k).padEnd(16)} -> ${extractLLMProvider(req)}`);\n      expect(extractLLMProvider(req)).toBe('cerebras');\n    }\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('C. if NaN DID escape, what reaches the licence server?', () => {\n  test('creditsForCost / estimateCreditsForCost clamp NaN and undefined', () => {\n    console.log('creditsForCost(NaN)               =', creditsForCost(NaN));\n    console.log('creditsForCost(undefined as any)  =', creditsForCost(undefined as unknown as number));\n    console.log('estimateCreditsForCost(NaN)       =', estimateCreditsForCost(NaN));\n    expect(creditsForCost(NaN)).toBe(0.1);\n    expect(creditsForCost(undefined as unknown as number)).toBe(0.1);\n  });\n\n  test('what the POST body would look like if the clamp were absent', () => {\n    console.log('JSON.stringify({ amount: NaN })       =', JSON.stringify({ amount: NaN
