DEFAULT` guard: they resolve THROUGH the\n// prototype chain to a truthy non-rate object, so `??` never fires.\nconst PROTO_KEYS = ['__proto__', 'constructor', 'toString', 'valueOf', 'hasOwnProperty'];\n\ndescribe('A. is the internal lookup NaN-able at all?', () => {\n  test('plain unknown model -> `?? default` fires, finite price', () => {\n    console.log('computeOpenAIChatCost(\"nonexistent-model\") =', computeOpenAIChatCost('nonexistent-model', usage));\n    console.log('computeGeminiChatCost(\"nonexistent-model\") =', computeGeminiChatCost('nonexistent-model', usage));\n    console.log('computeMistralChatCost(\"nonexistent-model\") =', computeMistralChatCost('nonexistent-model', usage));\n    expect(Number.isFinite(computeOpenAIChatCost('nonexistent-model', usage))).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  test('prototype-chain key DEFEATS the `??` default -> NaN (internal call only)', () => {\n    for (const k of PROTO_KEYS) {\n      const openai = computeOpenAIChatCost(k, usage);\n      const gemini = computeGeminiChatCost(k, usage);\n      const geminiStt = computeGeminiTranscriptionCost(k, { audioInputTokens: 1920, outputTokens: 100 });\n      const reservation = estimatePromptInputReservationUsd('gemini', k, 'x'.repeat(400));\n      console.log(\n        `key=${JSON.stringify(k).padEnd(16)} openaiChat=${openai} geminiChat=${gemini} geminiStt=${geminiStt} reservationUsd=${reservation}`\n      );\n    }\n    expect(Number.isNaN(computeOpenAIChatCost('__proto__', usage))).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('B. can a client-supplied field actually deliver that key?', () => {\n  test('POST /transcribe  X-STT-Provider -> isValidProviderId rejects prototype keys', () => {\n    for (const k of [...PROTO_KEYS, 'nonexistent']) {\n      console.log(`isValidProviderId(${JSON.stringify(k)}) =`, isValidProviderId(k));\n      expect(isValidProviderId(k)).toBe(false);\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('POST /transcribe  X-STT-Model / ?model= -> resolveModel rejects prototype keys (400)', () => {\n    for (const k of [...PROTO_KEYS, 'nonexistent']) {\n      const r = resolveModel('gemini', k);\n      console.log(`resolveModel(\"gemini\", ${JSON.stringify(k)}) =>`, JSON.stringify(r));\n      expect(r.ok).toBe(false);\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('estimatedUsdPerMinute is never reached with an unresolved model, and is finite anyway', () => {\n    for (const k of [...PROTO_KEYS, 'nonexistent']) {\n      const v = estimatedUsdPerMinute('gemini', k);\n      console.log(`estimatedUsdPerMinute(\"gemini\", ${JSON.stringify(k)}) =`, v);\n      expect(Number.isFinite(v)).toBe(true);\n      expect(v).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('POST /post-process  X-LLM-Model -> resolveLLMModel allowlists, prototype keys collapse to default', () => {\n    for (const k of [...PROTO_KEYS, 'nonexistent']) {\n      const req = new Request('https://x/post-process', { headers: { 'x-llm-provider': 'openai', 'x-llm-model': k } });\n      const provider = extractLLMProvider(req);\n      const model = resolveLLMModel(provider, req);\n      console.log(`x-llm-model: ${JSON.stringify(k).padEnd(16)} -> provider=${provider} resolvedModel=${model} cost=${computeOpenAIChatCost(model, usage)}`);\n      expect(model).toBe('gpt-5-mini');\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('X-LLM-Provider prototype key -> extractLLMProvider switch falls to default', () => {\n    for (const k of PROTO_KEYS) {\n      const req = new Request('https://x/post-process', { headers: { 'x-llm-provider': k } });\n      console.log(`x-llm-provider: ${JSON.stringify(k).padEnd(16)} -> ${extractLLMProvider(req)}`);\n      expect(extractLLMProvider(req)).toBe('cerebras');\n    }\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('C. if NaN DID escape, what reaches the licence server?', () => {\n  test('creditsForCost / estimateCreditsForCost clamp NaN and undefined', () => {\n    console.log('creditsForCost(NaN)               =', creditsForCost(NaN));\n    console.log('creditsForCost(undefined as any)  =', creditsForCost(undefined as unknown as number));\n    console.log('estimateCreditsForCost(NaN)       =', estimateCreditsForCost(NaN));\n    expect(creditsForCost(NaN)).toBe(0.1);\n    expect(creditsForCost(undefined as unknown as number)).toBe(0.1);\n  });\n\n  test('what the POST body would look like if the clamp were absent', () => {\n    console.log('JSON.stringify({ amount: NaN })       =', JSON.stringify({ amount: NaN }));\n    console.log('JSON.stringify({ amount: undefined }) =', JSON.stringify({ amount: undefined }));\n  });\n});\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ac43d8bd-42b0-4a58-83c5-75b70430595f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"8a0a00d8-a55e-44e1-aa7c-4ce553591ed4","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a18f2b8332658cbb6","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWHhwkq7bGvspNgTQkpz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"## What I found\n\n**Step 1 — where the key comes from.** Every flagged lookup is keyed on a *model id*, and every model id is allowlist-validated upstream before it reaches the arithmetic:\n\n- `/transcribe` — `X-STT-Provider` goes through `extractProvider` → `isValidProviderId` (a `Set` of `Object.keys(PROVIDERS)`, unknown ⇒ 400). `X-STT-Model` / `?model=` goes through `resolveModel` (`transcribe.ts:303-317`, unknown ⇒ 400), and only `modelResolution.model.id` — a registry literal — is passed onward (`transcribe.ts:318`). That same validated id is what reaches `estimatePromptInputReservationUsd` (`transcribe.ts:179`) and, via `ProviderRequestContext.model`, `computeGeminiTranscriptionCost` (`gemini.ts:168, 270`).\n- `/post-process` — `X-LLM-Model` goes through `resolveLLMModel` (`llm-provider.ts:110-117`), which is a strict array allowlist: `if (requested && config.allowed.includes(requested)) return requested; return config.default;`. Fallback paths use `defaultModelFor(provider)`. So `computeOpenAIChatCost` / `computeGeminiChatCost` / `computeMistralChatCost` only ever see 7 hardcoded ids.\n\n**Step 2 — the guard.** Every flagged lookup has an explicit `??` default onto a key that provably exists: `GEMINI_RATES[model] ?? GEMINI_FALLBACK_RATE` (296, 339) and `rates[model] ?? rates[defaultModel]` (436). I verified each default key is present in its map — `'gemini-2.5-flash'`, `'gpt-5-mini'`, `'mistral-small-latest'`. Same for `stt-models.ts:242/272/276/284`: `def.models.find(...) ?? def.models[0]`, and every provider has ≥1 model with its `defaultModel` present in the list.
